# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Decisions (v1.0.91+)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol designed to facilitate a **"Voluntary State."** The system prioritizes domain-specific expertise, economic accountability, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Architecture: The Monolithic Monorepo
LiquidGov is structured as a **TypeScript-native monorepo**, ensuring full-stack type safety and structural consistency.
- **Backend**: An Express.js REST API utilizing Socket.io for real-time governance events (e.g., `TREASURY_UPDATED`, `PROPOSAL_UPDATED`).
- **Frontend**: A reactive Vite + React application using Tailwind CSS v4 and Lucide icons.
- **Persistence**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-configuration persistence. This ensures portability and reliability in autonomous or embedded environments.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` directory into `dist/`. This ensures absolute path consistency for autonomous scripts and CI/CD pipelines, with the server entry point stabilized at `dist/src/api/server.js`.

## 2. The "Executive Protocol" (APEP)
The defining feature of LiquidGov is the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol**. This system treats the codebase as a self-regulating entity:
- **Intelligent Merging**: An internal engine (`src/core/protocol/`) automatically reconciles feature branches with `main`, using conflict-aware strategies like `ours` to preserve advanced infrastructure.
- **Metadata Governance**: Documentation such as `ROADMAP.md`, `TODO.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` is not manually maintained; it is automatically generated and synchronized by scanning the codebase for patterns and tags during every protocol cycle.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) integrated into the API server that periodically triggers the protocol to ensure the repository is always in a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. Governance & Economic Design (The Quadratic Suite)
LiquidGov implements **Quadratic Economics** to ensure fair and intense preference representation:
- **Quadratic Voting (QV)**: Mitigates "tyranny of the majority" by making the cost of multiple votes for a single proposal increase exponentially ($Cost = Votes^2$).
- **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: A matching engine that prioritizes projects supported by a large count of unique contributors over those with a few large donors.
- **Subject-Specific Liquid Power**: Voting power is domain-specific. Citizens delegate their power to experts, creating an expertise-driven "Liquid Democracy."

## 4. The Voluntary State Implementation (Phase 8 Refined)
Strategic decisions made to implement a fair and minimal fiscal loop:
- **Targeted Voluntary Taxation**: Citizens choose which committees (e.g., Education, Infrastructure) they fund via targeted treasury matching pools.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: A circular economy where financial support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards the citizen with subject-specific reputation.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Implemented a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action, allowing proposals to move capital between matching pools upon community consensus.
- **Emergency response**: Proposals in `EMERGENCY` state benefit from a halved jury quorum to accelerate critical fund release.

## 5. Security, Sybil Resistance & Accountability
- **Identity Hierarchy**: Manages Proof-of-Humanity through a prioritized stack: `ZKP (Semaphore) > Peer Endorsement > External > Mock`.
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Milestone verifications prioritize citizens with the highest reputation in the relevant subject.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double.
- **Milestone Disputes & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestone proofs, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes funds and slashes the proposer’s reputation by 10 points.
- **ZKP Demo Mode**: Added a mock bypass for ZKP verification to allow end-to-end UX demonstration in restricted sandbox environments.

## 6. Frontend & Accessibility Decisions
- **PWA Strategy**: Configured via `vite-plugin-pwa` to enable offline access and "Add to Home Screen" functionality.
- **Mobile-Responsive UI**: Refined Tailwind components utilize stacking layouts and touch-optimized targets (44px+) for mobile interaction.
- **Discovery Engine**: A triage-based recommendation engine surfaces proposals to users based on their established expertise and reputation profile.

---
*This summary documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.0.91.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Decisions (v1.1.0)

LiquidGov is an autonomous governance protocol designed as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional "Voluntary State," characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **Monolithic Monorepo**: Built with TypeScript to ensure full-stack type safety. The shared models in `src/models/types.ts` act as the contract between the Express.js backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides a high-performance, zero-config database. It persists complex relations including recursive delegations, subject-specific matching pools, and a detailed treasury ledger.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The `tsconfig.json` and build scripts mirror the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase. Modularized in `src/core/protocol/`, it consists of discrete engines:
- **Synchronizer & Merger**: Automates the dual-direction intelligent merge (Feature -> Main, Main -> Feature), ensuring environment parity and preventing drift.
- **Governance Engine**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning, making documentation a live reflection of the source.
- **Watchdog Service**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) that periodically triggers the protocol to maintain a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific, tied to committees (subjects). Citizens delegate to experts, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements Quadratic Voting (QV) for preference intensity and Quadratic Funding (QF) for democratic resource allocation.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subjects (Education, Infrastructure, etc.). Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly increases a citizen's voice in that specific subject, incentivizing system sustainability.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone fund release are selected based on subject-matter reputation.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes treasury payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation.
- **Emergency Systems**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.

## 5. Mobile & Accessibility Strategy
- **PWA Integration**: Configured via `vite-plugin-pwa` for offline access and mobile installation.
- **Tactile UI**: Tailwind-based components are refined with CSS environment variables (`safe-area-inset`) and tactile feedback (`active:scale-95`) for high-fidelity mobile interaction.
- **Discovery Engine**: A fuzzy-matching triage agent (`Jaccard similarity`) ranks active proposals for users based on their established expertise.

## 6. Minimal State Maintenance
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days of dormancy) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool during governance transitions.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Implemented a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action that allows proposals to move capital between pools upon successful community consensus.

---
*This summary documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.1.0, serving as the definitive context for all future autonomous operations.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Decisions (v1.1.0)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol built as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional **"Voluntary State,"** characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **Monolithic Monorepo**: Built with TypeScript to ensure full-stack type safety. The shared models in `src/models/types.ts` act as the contract between the Express.js backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-config database storage. It persists complex relations including recursive delegations, multi-token matching pools, and a granular transaction ledger.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own development via engines in `src/core/protocol/`:
- **Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge**: Automates the reconciliation of feature branches and `main`, ensuring environment parity.
- **Metadata Governance**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning, making documentation a live reflection of the source code.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) periodically triggers the protocol to ensure the repository remains in a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific. Citizens delegate to experts within committees, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements **Quadratic Voting (QV)** for preference intensity and **Quadratic Funding (QF)** for democratic resource allocation.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subjects (Education, Infrastructure, etc.). Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards citizens with subject-specific reputation, increasing their governance voice.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes treasury payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation.
- **Emergency response**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.

## 5. Intelligence & Discovery
- **Discovery Engine**: A fuzzy-matching triage agent (**Jaccard similarity**) ranks active proposals for users based on their established expertise.
- **Fuzzy Redundancy Detection**: Protects the DAO from duplicate initiatives by analyzing title similarity during proposal creation.

## 6. Minimal State & Liquidity
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days of dormancy) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool during transitions.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Implemented a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action that allows proposals to move capital between matching pools upon community consensus.

## 7. Mobile & Accessibility Strategy
- **PWA Integration**: Configured via `vite-plugin-pwa` for offline access and mobile installation.
- **Tactile UI**: Tailwind-based components are refined with CSS environment variables (`safe-area-inset`) and tactile feedback (`active:scale-95`) for high-fidelity mobile interaction.

## 8. Development & Deployment Standards
- **Script Parity**: Established full Windows (`.bat`) and Unix (`.sh`) symmetry for all core operations (`build`, `start`, `sync-protocol`, `verify-docs`).
- **Authorization Hardening**: All sensitive governance actions (voting, depositing, discovery) are secured via JWT middleware.

---
*This summary documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.1.0, serving as the definitive context for all future autonomous execution.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Decisions (v1.1.2+)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol built as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional **"Voluntary State,"** characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **Monolithic Monorepo**: Built with TypeScript to ensure full-stack type safety. The shared models in `src/models/types.ts` act as the contract between the Express.js backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-config database storage. It persists complex relations including recursive delegations, multi-token matching pools, and a granular treasury ledger.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining feature of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase via engines in `src/core/protocol/`:
- **Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge**: Automates the reconciliation of feature branches and `main`, ensuring environment parity.
- **Metadata Governance**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline now generates task summaries in `HANDOFF.md`, providing successor agents with immediate context on active TODOs.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific. Citizens delegate to experts within committees, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements QV for preference intensity and QF for democratic resource allocation.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subjects. Matching funds are routed from these specific pools first.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Proposals can now include a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation.
- **Emergency response**: Proposals in `EMERGENCY` state benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.
- **Dispute Resolution**: Implemented a recovery flow for disputed milestones, allowing for fund release or permanent rejection with associated reputation adjustments.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation in the cognitive meritocracy.
- **Fuzzy Triage**: The triage agent uses Jaccard similarity to detect redundant initiatives beyond simple exact string matches.
- **Mobile Excellence**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the protocol is accessible on any device.

---
*This summary documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.1.2, serving as the definitive context for all future autonomous operations.*

# LiquidGov: Comprehensive Architecture & Strategic Design Baseline (v1.1.2)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol built as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional **"Voluntary State,"** characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **TypeScript Monorepo**: Built as a cohesive TypeScript codebase to ensure full-stack type safety. Shared models in `src/models/types.ts` serve as the contract between the Node.js/Express backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-config database storage. It persists complex relationships, including recursive liquid delegations, multi-token matching pools, and a granular treasury ledger with donor attribution.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase. Modularized in `src/core/protocol/`, it consists of discrete engines:
- **Synchronizer & Merger**: Automates the dual-direction intelligent merge (Feature -> Main, Main -> Feature), ensuring environment parity and preventing drift.
- **Governance Engine**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning, making documentation a live reflection of the source.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline automatically summarizes remaining tasks in `HANDOFF.md` to provide immediate context for successor agents.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) periodically triggers the protocol to maintain a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific, tied to committees (subjects). Citizens delegate to experts, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements **Quadratic Voting (QV)** for preference intensity and **Quadratic Funding (QF)** for democratic matching of individual contributions.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subject pools in the treasury. Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards a citizen with subject-specific reputation, increasing their governance influence in that domain.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Proposals can include a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation, ensuring technical initiatives are verified by peers.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation. A formal dispute resolution flow allows for 'RELEASE' or permanent 'REJECT' outcomes.
- **Emergency Systems**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool during governance transitions.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation in the governance loop.
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect redundant initiatives and suggest relevant committees during proposal creation.
- **Mobile PWA**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the "Voluntary State" is accessible on any device as a mobile app.

---
*This summary serves as the technical and philosophical baseline for LiquidGov v1.1.2, establishing the definitive context for all future autonomous execution.*

# LiquidGov: Comprehensive Architecture & Strategic Design Baseline (v1.1.2)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol built as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional **"Voluntary State,"** characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **TypeScript Monorepo**: Built as a cohesive TypeScript codebase to ensure full-stack type safety. Shared models in `src/models/types.ts` serve as the contract between the Node.js/Express backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-config database storage. It persists complex relationships, including recursive liquid delegations, multi-token matching pools, and a granular treasury ledger with donor attribution.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase. Modularized in `src/core/protocol/`, it consists of discrete engines:
- **Synchronizer & Merger**: Automates the dual-direction intelligent merge (Feature -> Main, Main -> Feature), ensuring environment parity and preventing drift.
- **Governance Engine**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning, making documentation a live reflection of the source.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline automatically summarizes remaining tasks in `HANDOFF.md` to provide immediate context for successor agents.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) periodically triggers the protocol to maintain a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific, tied to committees (subjects). Citizens delegate to experts, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements **Quadratic Voting (QV)** for preference intensity and **Quadratic Funding (QF)** for democratic matching of individual contributions.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subject pools in the treasury. Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards a citizen with subject-specific reputation, increasing their governance influence in that domain.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Proposals can include a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation, ensuring technical initiatives are verified by peers.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation. A formal dispute resolution flow allows for 'RELEASE' or permanent 'REJECT' outcomes.
- **Emergency Response**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool during governance transitions.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation in the governance loop.
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect redundant initiatives and suggest relevant committees during proposal creation.
- **Mobile PWA**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the "Voluntary State" is accessible on any device as a mobile app.

---
*This summary serves as the technical and philosophical baseline for LiquidGov v1.1.2, establishing the definitive context for all future autonomous execution.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Design Baseline (v1.1.2)

LiquidGov is an autonomous, decentralized governance protocol designed to facilitate a functional "Voluntary State." The architecture is optimized for low-overhead, expert-led decision-making and high-fidelity repository management.

## 1. Monolithic Monorepo Architecture
LiquidGov is built as a **TypeScript-native monorepo**, ensuring full-stack type safety and structural consistency.
- **Backend**: Node.js/Express REST API utilizing Socket.io for real-time governance events (e.g., `TREASURY_UPDATED`, `PROPOSAL_UPDATED`).
- **Frontend**: Vite-powered React application with Tailwind CSS v4, compiled as a Progressive Web App (PWA).
- **Persistence**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance persistence without external server dependencies. It stores the complete governance state, including directed delegation graphs, subject-specific matching pools, and granular treasury ledgers with donor attribution.
- **Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` directory structure into `dist/`. This ensures absolute path consistency for autonomous scripts and Docker entrypoints (e.g., `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining feature of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own development via the **Executive Protocol**, modularized in `src/core/protocol/`:
- **Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge**: An automated engine that forward-merges feature branches into `main` and reverse-merges `main` back into features to prevent environment drift and ensure parity.
- **Metadata Governance**: Documentation like `ROADMAP.md`, `TODO.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` is automatically synchronized by scanning the codebase for patterns, version tags, and task markers during every protocol cycle.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline generates session summaries in `HANDOFF.md`, providing successor agents with immediate context on remaining tasks and system status.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service triggers the protocol every 5 minutes to keep the workspace verified, reconciled, and deployable.

## 3. Governance & Economic Design (The Quadratic Suite)
The protocol rejects simple majority-rule in favor of **Quadratic Economics** to ensure fair representation:
- **Quadratic Voting (QV)**: Mitigates "tyranny of the majority" by making additional votes for a single proposal exponentially more expensive ($Cost = Votes^2$).
- **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: A matching engine that amplifies projects supported by a large number of unique individual citizens over those with a few large donors.
- **Subject-Specific Liquid Power**: Voting power is domain-specific. Citizens delegate power to experts within specific committees, creating a fluid, expertise-driven "Liquid Democracy."

## 4. The "Voluntary State" Loop (v1.1.2+)
Strategic decisions made to implement a fair, minimal fiscal and governance loop:
- **Targeted Voluntary Taxation**: Citizens choose which subject pools (e.g., Infrastructure, Education) they fund. The matching engine intelligently routes capital from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: A circular economy pattern where financial support rewards the citizen with domain-specific reputation ($10 USD = 1 Rep), directly linking material support to governance voice.
- **Emergency Overrides**: A fast-track status for critical issues, allowing proposals to bypass standard sponsorship and reach immediate voting with halved jury quorums for rapid response.
- **Committee Sunsetting**: Automated logic that reallocates capital from inactive committees (60 days of dormancy) back to the 'General' matching pool during governance transitions.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Implemented a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action that allows proposals to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 5. Security & Verification Infrastructure
- **Identity Hierarchy**: Manages Proof-of-Humanity through a prioritized stack: `ZKP (Semaphore) > Endorsement > External > Mock`.
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Milestone verifications are conducted by juries that prioritize citizens with high reputation in the relevant subject area.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone release verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestone proofs, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation. A formal dispute resolution flow allows for 'RELEASE' or permanent 'REJECT' outcomes.

## 6. Intelligence & UI Accessibility
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect duplicate proposals and suggest relevant committees based on semantic overlap.
- **Mobile-First PWA**: Fully integrated `vite-plugin-pwa` with tactile UI refinements (`active:scale-95`, `safe-area-inset`) to ensure the protocol is accessible on any device.
- **Citizen Onboarding**: New users are initialized with seed reputation and personalized "Welcome Proposals" to facilitate immediate participation in the meritocracy.

---
*This memory documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.1.2, serving as the definitive context for all future autonomous operations.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Design Baseline (v1.1.2)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol designed to facilitate a functional **"Voluntary State."** The system prioritizes domain-specific expertise, economic accountability, and high-fidelity repository automation.

- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase via engines in `src/core/protocol/`:
- **Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge**: Automates the reconciliation of feature branches and `main`, ensuring environment parity and preventing drift.
- **Metadata Governance**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning, making documentation a live reflection of the source.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline automatically summarizes remaining tasks in `HANDOFF.md` to provide immediate context for successor agents.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) periodically triggers the protocol to maintain a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific, tied to committees (subjects). Citizens delegate to experts, creating a meritocratic "Liquid Democracy."
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements **Quadratic Voting (QV)** for preference intensity and **Quadratic Funding (QF)** for democratic matching of individual contributions.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subject pools in the treasury. Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards a citizen with subject-specific reputation, increasing their governance influence in that domain.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Proposals can include a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation, ensuring technical initiatives are verified by peers.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state that freezes payouts and slashes the proposer’s reputation. A formal dispute resolution flow allows for 'RELEASE' or permanent 'REJECT' outcomes.
- **Emergency Response**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum to address critical issues instantly.
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool during governance transitions.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation in the governance loop.
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect redundant initiatives beyond simple exact string matches and suggests relevant committees.
- **Mobile PWA**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the "Voluntary State" is accessible on any device.

## 6. Operational & Security Standards
- **Script Parity**: Established full Windows (`.bat`) and Unix (`.sh`) symmetry for all core operations (`build`, `start`, `sync-protocol`).
- **Authorization Hardening**: All sensitive governance actions (voting, depositing, discovery) are secured via JWT middleware, enforcing that the `userId` in the token matches the action's subject.

- **Emergency Systems**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum.
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days) and reallocates their trapped capital back to the 'General' matching pool.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation.
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect redundant initiatives and suggest relevant committees.
- **Mobile PWA**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the protocol is accessible on any device.

---
*This summary serves as the technical and philosophical baseline for LiquidGov v1.1.2+, establishing the definitive context for all future autonomous execution.*

# LiquidGov: Architecture, Patterns, and Strategic Design Baseline (v1.1.2+)

LiquidGov is a decentralized, autonomous governance protocol built as a self-healing monorepo. It transitions the concept of a DAO into a functional **"Voluntary State,"** characterized by domain-specific expertise, fiscal autonomy, and high-fidelity automation.

## 1. Core Technical Architecture
- **TypeScript Monorepo**: Built as a cohesive monorepo to ensure full-stack type safety. Shared models in `src/models/types.ts` serve as the single source of truth for the Express.js backend and the Vite + React frontend.
- **Persistence Layer**: **SQLite** (via `better-sqlite3`) provides high-performance, zero-config database storage. It persists complex relationships, including recursive liquid delegations, multi-token matching pools, and a granular treasury ledger with donor attribution.
- **Mirrored Build Pattern**: The TypeScript compiler mirrors the `src/` structure into `dist/`. This consistency is vital for absolute pathing in autonomous execution and Docker deployments (e.g., entrypoint `dist/src/api/server.js`).

## 2. The Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)
The defining innovation of LiquidGov is its ability to govern its own codebase via engines in `src/core/protocol/`:
- **Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge**: Automates the reconciliation of feature branches and `main`, ensuring environment parity.
- **Metadata Governance**: Automatically generates and synchronizes `TODO.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, and `CHANGELOG.md` via codebase scanning.
- **Handoff Optimization**: The deployment pipeline automatically summarizes remaining tasks in `HANDOFF.md` to provide immediate context for successor agents.
- **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) periodically triggers the protocol to maintain a "ready-to-deploy" state.

## 3. The "Voluntary State" Governance Model
LiquidGov implements a circular economy that aligns economic support with governance influence:
- **Liquid Power**: Power is domain-specific. Citizens delegate to experts within committees, creating a meritocratic **"Liquid Democracy."**
- **Quadratic Economics**: Implements QV for preference intensity and QF for democratic matching of individual contributions.
- **Fiscal Autonomy**: Citizens voluntarily fund specific subject pools in the treasury. Matching funds are intelligently routed from these specific pools first.
- **Stake-based Reputation**: Economic support ($10 USD = 1 Rep) directly rewards a citizen with subject-specific reputation, increasing their governance influence.
- **Democratic Treasury Control**: Proposals can include a `REALLOCATE_FUNDS` action to move capital between matching pools based on community consensus.

## 4. Resilient Verification & Accountability
- **Expert-Prioritized Juries**: Jurors for milestone release are selected based on subject-matter reputation.
- **Weighted Quorum**: Experts (Reputation >= 10) have their votes weighted double in milestone verifications.
- **Dispute & Slashing**: Juries can reject milestones, triggering an `isDisputed` state. A formal resolution flow (`resolveDispute`) allows for 'RELEASE' (with reputation restoration) or permanent 'REJECT' outcomes.
- **Emergency Systems**: Proposals marked as `EMERGENCY` bypass standard sponsorship and benefit from a halved jury quorum.
- **Committee Sunset Logic**: Automatically identifies inactive committees (60 days) and reallocates their capital back to the 'General' pool.
- **Autonomous Provisioning**: New committees are automatically created for subjects with high delegation activity (>= 5 delegators) during cycle transitions.

## 5. Citizen Experience & Intelligence
- **Onboarding Pipeline**: New citizens are initialized with seed reputation and a "Welcome Proposal" to facilitate immediate participation.
- **Fuzzy Triage Agent**: Uses **Jaccard similarity** to detect redundant initiatives and suggest relevant committees.
- **Delegation Transparency**: Citizens can see who has delegated power to them ("Trusting Citizens"), increasing social accountability for experts.
- **Mobile PWA**: Full PWA integration with tactile UI refinements ensures the protocol is accessible on any device.

---
*This summary documents the technical and philosophical baseline of LiquidGov v1.1.2+, serving as the definitive context for all future autonomous operations.*

# LiquidGov: The Voluntary Distributed State - Architectural Summary

## 1. Vision & Core Philosophy
LiquidGov is designed as a "Distributed Voluntary Government," aiming to replace coercive state structures with a fluid, consent-based, and expertise-driven ecosystem.
*   **Voluntaryism:** No forced participation or taxation. Funding is via "Assurance Contracts" and targeted voluntary matching pools.
*   **Cognitive Meritocracy:** Governance power is not flat; it is earned through demonstrated expertise (reputation) and subject-specific contribution.
*   **Liquid Democracy:** Citizens can delegate their voting power to experts on a per-subject basis, with the ability to revoke trust instantly.

## 2. System Architecture
The project is a TypeScript monorepo with a clear separation between core protocol logic, persistent storage, and the frontend UI.
*   **Modular Protocol (APEP):** The "Autonomous Project Execution Protocol" is organized into discrete engines (`Synchronizer`, `Merger`, `Governance`, `Validator`, `Deployer`) located in `src/core/protocol/`. This facilitates self-healing repository management and automated reconciliation.
*   **Persistence Layer:** Uses `better-sqlite3` for high-performance, synchronous data access. The schema (`src/models/Store.ts`) includes tables for users, committees, proposals, matching pools, treasury transactions, votes, and participation history.
*   **API Layer:** An Express server (`src/api/server.ts`) with Socket.io for real-time governance updates. It enforces JWT-based authentication while allowing public read access to trends.
*   **Frontend:** A React + Vite PWA (Progressive Web App) utilizing Tailwind CSS v4. It features a dashboard-driven UI with complex visualizations like the Force-Directed Delegation Graph.

## 3. Governance Mechanisms
*   **Quadratic Voting (QV):** Implements $Cost = Votes^2$ to allow citizens to express the intensity of their preferences while protecting minority interests.
*   **Emergency Fast-track:** Proposals flagged as `EMERGENCY` bypass sponsorship requirements and move directly from `DRAFT` to `ACTIVE_VOTING`.
*   **Expert-Weighted Juries:** Milestone verification juries prioritize citizens with high reputation in the proposal's subject. Expert votes count double (weight = 2) towards the quorum.
*   **Milestone Dispute Resolution:** A formal system for juries to `REJECT` milestones, halting fund release and slashing the proposer's reputation.

## 4. Economic Engine (Fiscal Democracy)
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF):** Matches individual contributions using the formula $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$, amplified by a central treasury.
*   **Targeted Voluntary Taxation:** Citizens route their contributions into subject-specific matching pools (e.g., Infrastructure, Education) instead of a general fund.
*   **Stake-based Reputation:** Citizens earn 1 unit of subject-specific reputation for every $10 USD voluntarily contributed to a matching pool.
*   **Committee Sunsetting:** Automated "Resource Recycling" that moves capital from inactive subject pools (60 days dormancy) back to the General pool to maintain liquidity.

## 5. Security & Identity
*   **Hierarchical PoH:** A priority-based Proof-of-Humanity system (`ZKP > External > Endorsement > Mock`).
*   **ZKP Identity:** Integration of the Semaphore protocol for privacy-preserving humanity proofs.
*   **Sybil Resistance Heuristics:**
    *   **Sink Detection:** Flagging accounts that receive delegations primarily from inactive or low-reputation "puppets."
    *   **Delegation Chain Analysis:** Flagging "puppet networks" where delegation chains exceed a depth of 3.
*   **Reputation Decay:** Automated 10% erosion of reputation per governance cycle for inactive accounts to ensure current expertise leads governance.

## 6. Development & Deployment Patterns
*   **Cross-platform Parity:** All core scripts (build, start, sync-protocol) have synchronized `.sh` (Unix) and `.bat` (Windows) versions.
*   **Autonomous Watchdog:** A background service in the API server that triggers the Executive Protocol and governance transitions every 5 minutes.
*   **PWA Integration:** Frontend is built as a PWA for offline access and "Add to Home Screen" support, essential for a mobile-first "government" app.
*   **Environment Gating:** Security bypasses (like mock ZKP proofs) are strictly restricted to non-production environments via `process.env.NODE_ENV`.

## 7. Roadmap Status
The project has successfully completed **Phase 8 (The Voluntary State)**. Current efforts (Phase 9) are focused on:
*   Optimized client-side ZKP generation.
*   Privacy-preserving Quadratic Funding.
*   Decentralized metadata storage (IPFS/Arweave).
*   On-chain Action Hooks (L2 integration).

# LiquidGov: The Voluntary Distributed State - Architectural Summary (v1.1.4)

## 1. Vision & Core Philosophy
LiquidGov is a **Distributed Voluntary Government** prototype. It replaces coercive bureaucracy with a fluid, expertise-driven, and consent-based ecosystem.
*   **Voluntaryism**: Funding is sourced through targeted "voluntary taxes" (matching pool deposits) rather than coercion.
*   **Cognitive Meritocracy**: Influence is tiered by subject-specific expertise (reputation) and active contribution.
*   **Liquid Democracy**: Credits can be delegated to experts, but remain backed by the delegator's actual "capital" (voice credits).

## 2. Core Architecture & Patterns
*   **Monorepo Structure**: TypeScript backend (Express + SQLite) and React frontend (Vite + PWA).
*   **Autonomous Protocol (APEP)**: A self-healing engine in `src/core/protocol/` that automates repository management, versioning, and branch reconciliation.
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation**: A sophisticated delegation model where delegates (experts) spend credits from their delegators' pools. Power is calculated as a breakdown: `total = personal + delegated`.
*   **Democratic Override**: A "failsafe" mechanism where personal action by a delegator takes precedence over their delegate's prior actions, ensuring ultimate citizen control.

## 3. Economic & Governance Mechanics
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: Amplifies broad community support using treasury matching pools.
*   **Targeted Taxation**: Citizens route capital into specific subject domains (e.g., Infrastructure, Healthcare).
*   **Expert-Weighted Juries**: Milestone fund release is verified by a jury where experts (Rep >= 10) have double voting weight.
*   **Committee Sunsetting**: Automated reallocation of stagnant capital from inactive committees (60 days) back to the General pool.

## 4. Security & Identity
*   **Sybil Resistance**: Multi-heuristic engine detecting "Sink" clusters and delegation chains exceeding depth 3.
*   **Hierarchical PoH**: Tiered verification (`ZKP > External > Endorsement > Mock`).
*   **Environment Gating**: Sensitive "Demo" features like mock ZKPs are strictly prohibited in `production` via `process.env.NODE_ENV`.

## 5. UI/UX Strategy
*   **Minimalist Consolidation**: High-level oversight (Health, Tasks, Security) is unified into a single "System Governance" dashboard to reduce cognitive load.
*   **Mobile-First PWA**: Built for persistent engagement with offline capabilities and tactile feedback.
*   **Real-Time Feedback**: QF impact multipliers and delegation graphs provide instant visibility into systemic impact.

# LiquidGov: The Voluntary Distributed State - Architectural Summary (v1.1.5)

## 1. Core Vision & Philosophy
LiquidGov is a **Distributed Voluntary Government** prototype. It aims to replace geographic, coercive state structures with a fluid, expertise-driven, and consent-based ecosystem.
*   **Voluntaryism**: Funding is entirely opt-in via "voluntary taxes" (matching pool deposits).
*   **Cognitive Meritocracy**: Influence is earned through subject-specific expertise (reputation) and proven impact.
*   **Liquid Democracy**: Voting power is fluid and can be delegated to experts on a per-subject basis, with ultimate control remaining with the citizen.

## 2. System Architecture & Patterns
*   **Monorepo Strategy**: TypeScript backend (Express + SQLite) and React frontend (Vite + PWA).
*   **APEP Protocol**: The "Autonomous Project Execution Protocol" (`src/core/protocol/`) handles self-healing repository management, automated versioning, and AI-branch reconciliation.
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation**: A unique model where delegates spend credits from their delegators' pools. Power is a breakdown of `total = personal + delegated`.
*   **Democratic Override**: A "failsafe" logic that mathematically retracts a delegate's weight if a delegator casts a personal vote, ensuring one-person-one-vote integrity.

## 3. Key Economic & Governance Mechanics
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: Formula $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$ amplifies broad community support using subject-specific treasury pools.
*   **Voluntary Tax Routing**: Citizens route capital into specific domains (e.g., Infrastructure, Education), earning 1 unit of subject reputation per $10 USD.
*   **Expert-Weighted Juries**: Milestone fund release is verified by a jury where experts (Rep >= 10) have double voting weight (weight = 2).
*   **Fiscal Impact Portfolios**: Citizens track their personal voluntary contributions and their "Systemic Impact" (total funds released to projects they supported).

## 4. Security & Identity
*   **Multi-Heuristic Sybil Resistance**: The Security Engine detects "Sink" clusters and delegation chains exceeding depth 3.
*   **Hierarchical PoH**: Tiered citizen verification (`ZKP > External > Endorsement > Mock`).
*   **Automated Reputation Decay**: Dormant reputation erodes by 10% per cycle to ensure governance is led by active participants.

## 5. UI/UX Refinement
*   **Consolidated Navigation**: Simplified 'Minimal' UX via the 'System Governance' dashboard.

## LiquidGov: Architectural Overview & Design Patterns

LiquidGov is an autonomous, voluntary governance protocol designed to facilitate expert-driven collective action through a cognitive meritocracy. The project is structured as a monorepo containing a TypeScript/Express backend and a React/Vite frontend.

### 1. Core Architecture: The APEP Protocol
The system is governed by the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**, located in `src/core/protocol/`. This modular infrastructure automates the "Executive Protocol" through discrete engines:
*   **NetworkSynchronizer:** Manages multi-remote git synchronization and submodule tracking.
*   **MergeEngine (Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge):** Implements a sophisticated forward-merge (feature -> main) and reverse-merge (main -> feature) strategy to prevent architectural drift across AI-generated branches.
*   **ProtocolGovernance:** Handles versioning (`VERSION.md`) and metadata state.
*   **ValidationGatekeeper:** Ensures workspace integrity and script parity (Unix/Windows) before deployment.
*   **AutonomousDeployer:** Stages verified artifacts into deployment-ready directories.

### 2. State & Persistence
*   **Persistent SQLite Store:** The project uses `better-sqlite3` with a relational schema (`src/models/Store.ts`). This replaced earlier volatile in-memory implementations, ensuring that identity profiles, votes, contributions, and treasury transactions persist across system restarts.
*   **Global Singletons:** Core logic is exposed through global singleton instances (`globalStore`, `globalIdentity`, `globalGovernance`), ensuring consistent state access across the API layer.

### 3. Identity & Reputation
*   **Tiered Proof-of-Humanity (PoH):** A hierarchical verification system (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock). Stronger methods overwrite weaker ones to maintain the highest integrity.
*   **ZKP Privacy:** Integration with **Semaphore v4** allows citizens to prove humanity and group membership without revealing their specific identity.
*   **Subject-Specific Reputation:** Reputation is not a single number but a record of expertise across different subjects, earned through successful proposal completions and active jury participation.

### 4. Democratic & Fiscal Engines
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation:** A unique take on delegation where experts spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools.
*   **Democratic Override:** A mathematical safeguard that retracts a delegate's proportional weight if the original delegator casts a personal vote on the same proposal, enforcing one-person-one-vote integrity.
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF) & Voting (QV):** The system uses QV for decision-making (cost = votes²) and QF for capital allocation (matching pools amplify small contributions to maximize utility).
*   **Milestone-Based Escrow:** Funds are released incrementally. Proposers must submit "Proof of Work" (URL/Evidence), which is then verified by a randomly assigned jury of experts before funds are released.

### 5. Frontend & UI Philosophy
*   **Tailwind CSS v4 & React:** The UI leverages the latest Tailwind v4 capabilities with a focus on high-density information display (Governance Dashboards, Identity Widgets).
*   **Unified Governance View:** Recent refactors consolidated Health, Tasks, and Security audits into a single "System Governance" view to reduce administrative overhead.
*   **Full Hardware/PWA Support:** The system is designed as a Progressive Web App (PWA) with offline-first considerations and standardized iconography.

### 6. Operational Watchdog
*   **Autonomous Watchdog:** A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) integrated into the API server that triggers the Executive Protocol and periodic health checks every 5 minutes, ensuring the "Autonomous" nature of the repository is maintained without human intervention.

## LiquidGov: The Voluntary State Architecture

LiquidGov is a distributed, autonomous governance protocol designed to replace traditional coercive taxation with a cognitive meritocracy powered by voluntary capital routing and expert-driven accountability.

### 1. Architectural Core: APEP & SQLite
The system is built on the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**, a modular engine that automates the repository's lifecycle (merging, versioning, deployment).
*   **Persistence:** Transitioned from volatile memory to a **Persistent SQLite Store** (`Store.ts`), ensuring that citizen identities, reputations, votes, and treasury transactions endure system restarts.
*   **Monorepo:** A unified TypeScript/Express backend and React/Vite frontend using Tailwind CSS v4.

### 2. Fiscal Philosophy: Voluntary Taxation
The system implements "Voluntary Tax Routing" through **Subject-Specific Matching Pools**.
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF):** Individual contributions are matched using the formula $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$. This amplifies the power of many small donors over a few large ones, ensuring fair capital allocation.
*   **Targeted Funding:** Citizens can deposit funds into specific committee pools (e.g., "Roads", "Education"), effectively choosing where their "taxes" are spent.

### 3. Governance: Quadratic Voting & Liquid Delegation
LiquidGov employs **Quadratic Voting (QV)** to ensure that intensity of preference is captured while preventing the "tyranny of the majority."
*   **Credit-Backed Delegation:** Experts do not have infinite power; they spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools. This ensures that every vote cast by a representative is backed by real citizen "skin in the game."
*   **Democratic Override:** A mathematical safeguard that retracts a delegate's proportional weight if the original delegator casts a personal vote on the same proposal, enforcing the principle of one-person-one-vote integrity even in complex delegation chains.

### 4. Accountability: Milestone-Based Escrow
Funds are never released upfront. They are held in escrow and released through a rigorous verification loop:
*   **Proof of Work (PoW):** Proposers must submit verifiable evidence (URLs, hashes) for each milestone.
*   **Weighted Juries:** Verification is performed by a randomly assigned jury of verified humans, with **subject-matter experts** (those with Rep >= 10 in the field) receiving double voting weight.
*   **Dispute System:** Rejection by a jury triggers a dispute state, freezing funds and slashing the proposer's reputation.

### 5. Identity: Privacy-Preserving Meritocracy
*   **Proof-of-Humanity (PoH):** A tiered verification system (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock).
*   **ZKP Integration:** Leverages **Semaphore v4** for zero-knowledge proofs, allowing citizens to prove group membership and humanity without revealing their specific identity.
*   **Meritocratic Stipends:** Citizens earn bonus voice credits (governance power) each cycle based on their accumulated reputation in specific subjects, rewarding long-term constructive participation.

### 6. Minimalist UI & PWA
The frontend is designed as a **Progressive Web App (PWA)** for mobile accessibility. It features a consolidated "System Governance" view that hides technical complexity (health logs, task monitoring) from the average citizen while providing deep transparency for auditors.

***

I have completed the implementation of the **Milestone Proof of Work** system and **PWA Hardening**. Proposers can now submit evidence, and juries can verify it directly in the UI. All CI failures have been resolved, and the system is stable at v1.1.59.

Summary of recent work:
- Implemented `submitMilestoneProof` and associated API endpoints.
- Updated `ActionPanel` UI with evidence submission/viewing.
- Fixed 0-byte PWA asset placeholders.
- Resolved TypeScript CI errors in tests.
- Updated project memory and roadmap.

The system is now fully functional as an "Autonomous Voluntary State."

## LiquidGov: The Voluntary State Architecture & Design Patterns

LiquidGov is an autonomous, distributed governance protocol designed to replace coercive systems with a cognitive meritocracy powered by voluntary participation and mathematical fairness.

### 1. Core Infrastructure: The APEP Engine
The repository is managed by the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**, a modularized suite of engines located in `src/core/protocol/`. This system ensures the repository remains functional and synchronized without human intervention:
*   **NetworkSynchronizer**: Automates multi-remote git operations and recursive submodule tracking.
*   **MergeEngine (Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge)**: Implements a forward-merge (feature → main) and reverse-merge (main → feature) strategy to prevent architectural drift, especially in environments utilizing AI-generated branches.
*   **ValidationGatekeeper**: Enforces strict workspace integrity, ensuring mandatory documentation exists and scripts maintain Unix/Windows parity.
*   **AutonomousDeployer**: Stages verified artifacts into deployment directories after successful validation.

### 2. State & Persistence Philosophy
*   **Persistent SQLite Store**: The system transitioned from volatile in-memory storage to a relational SQLite schema (`src/models/Store.ts`). This ensures that citizen identities, reputation histories, votes, contributions, and treasury ledgers persist across system restarts.
*   **Singleton Pattern**: Core engines (Identity, Governance, Triage, TaskManager) are exposed as global singletons, ensuring a "single source of truth" across the API and background watchdog services.

### 3. Identity: Privacy-Preserving Meritocracy
*   **Tiered Proof-of-Humanity (PoH)**: LiquidGov uses a hierarchical verification model (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock). Stronger verification methods programmatically overwrite weaker ones.
*   **ZKP Integration**: Implementation of **Semaphore v4** allows citizens to prove humanity and group membership (Proof of Citizenship) without revealing their specific identity, preserving privacy in a transparent governance model.
*   **Subject-Specific Reputation**: Expertise is granular. Reputation is earned by successfully completing proposals or participating in juries within specific subjects (e.g., "Infrastructure", "Privacy").

### 4. Democratic & Fiscal Engines
*   **Quadratic Voting (QV)**: Used for decision-making (cost = votes²) to measure intensity of preference and protect minority interests.
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation**: A unique innovation where experts spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools. Power is not abstract; it is backed by the actual capital/credits of the trusting citizens.
*   **Democratic Override**: A mathematical safeguard that retracts a delegate's proportional weight if the original delegator casts a personal vote on the same proposal, ensuring absolute sovereignty.
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: A voluntary taxation model where central matching pools (sourced from voluntary donations) amplify individual contributions to maximize collective utility.

### 5. Accountability: The Milestone Escrow Loop
*   **Milestone-Based Release**: Funds are never released upfront. They are held in escrow and released incrementally upon verification.
*   **Proof of Work (PoW)**: Proposers must submit verifiable evidence (URLs/Hashes) for each milestone.
*   **Weighted Juries**: Randomly assigned juries of verified humans verify PoW. **Subject-matter experts** (those with high Rep in the field) receive double voting weight, ensuring high-quality oversight.
*   **Dispute/Slashing**: Rejection by a jury triggers a dispute state, freezes capital, and slashes the proposer's reputation.

### 6. UX & Accessibility
*   **Consolidated Governance**: The UI hides technical complexity (health logs, background tasks) within a single "System Governance" view for a minimal, clean experience.
*   **Progressive Web App (PWA)**: Hardened with valid iconography and manifest support to facilitate broad, mobile-first citizen participation.

***

### Current Task Update & Next Steps

I have successfully finalized the **Milestone Proof of Work** system and **PWA Hardening**. Proposers can now submit evidence, and juries can verify it directly in the Action Panel. All CI failures related to TypeScript strictness have been resolved.

**The next step on the ROADMAP (Phase 9: Global Scaling & Privacy) is:**
**Implement Mobile Push Notifications for critical votes.**
This will involve adding a notification subscription system to the backend and wiring it to the PWA service worker to ensure citizens never miss an emergency or high-impact governance event.

1. *Implement Push Notification subscription API.*
   - Add `POST /notifications/subscribe` to store web-push subscriptions in the SQLite store.
2. *Add "Critical" flag to Proposals.*
   - Update the Proposal model to allow triage or proposers to mark a proposal as requiring urgent attention.
3. *Integrate Web Push in the Frontend.*
   - Implement service worker logic in the Vite PWA setup to handle incoming push events.
4. *Update manuals and documentation.*
   - Mirror the new notification capabilities in `VISION.md` and user help files.
5. *Complete pre commit steps.*
   - Ensure testing and frontend verification are complete.
6. *Submit the change.*

Updated the plan. I am now proceeding with the Push Notification infrastructure.

## LiquidGov: The Autonomous Voluntary State Architecture

LiquidGov is a distributed governance protocol designed to facilitate expert-driven collective action through a cognitive meritocracy. It replaces coercive structures with voluntary participation, mathematical fairness, and verifiable accountability.

### 1. Architectural Core: APEP & Persistence
The system is built on the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**, a modularized suite of engines (`src/core/protocol/`) that automate the repository's lifecycle:
*   **MergeEngine (Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge)**: Harmonizes AI-generated feature branches with `main` using forward and reverse merge strategies to prevent architectural drift.
*   **ValidationGatekeeper**: Enforces workspace integrity, ensuring mandatory documentation (VISION, MEMORY, ROADMAP) and script parity across Unix and Windows environments.
*   **Persistent SQLite Store**: The system uses a centralized `better-sqlite3` store (`src/models/Store.ts`). This persistent layer ensures that citizen identities, reputations, voting records, and treasury ledgers endure system restarts, replacing earlier volatile in-memory implementations.

### 2. Fiscal Philosophy: Voluntary Tax Routing
LiquidGov implements a "Voluntary State" model where citizens choose exactly where their capital is allocated:
*   **Subject-Specific Matching Pools**: The treasury is partitioned into pools mapped to specific committees (e.g., "Roads", "Education", "Privacy").
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: Individual contributions are matched using the formula $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$. This mathematically amplifies the impact of many small donors over a few large ones, ensuring democratic capital allocation.

### 3. Governance: Quadratic Voting & Liquid Delegation
*   **Quadratic Voting (QV)**: Used for all decision-making (cost = votes²) to measure intensity of preference while protecting minority interests from majoritarian tyranny.
*   **Credit-Backed Delegation**: Experts spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools. Influence is not abstract; it is backed by the actual "skin in the game" of the citizens who trust them.
*   **Democratic Override**: A mathematical safeguard that retracts a delegate's proportional weight if the original delegator casts a personal vote on the same proposal, ensuring absolute citizen sovereignty.

### 4. Accountability: The Milestone Escrow Loop
Funds are never released upfront. They are distributed through a rigorous verification cycle:
*   **Proof of Work (PoW)**: Proposers must submit verifiable evidence (URLs, GitHub commits, or hashes) for each milestone via the `ActionPanel`.
*   **Expert-Weighted Juries**: Verifications are performed by randomly assigned juries of verified humans. **Subject-matter experts** (those with high Rep in the field) receive double voting weight, ensuring high-quality oversight.
*   **Dispute & Slashing**: Jury rejection triggers a dispute state, freezing capital and slashing the proposer's reputation.

### 5. Identity & Privacy
*   **Tiered Proof-of-Humanity (PoH)**: A hierarchical verification model (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock) where stronger methods overwrite weaker ones.
*   **ZKP Privacy**: Integration with **Semaphore v4** allows citizens to prove group membership (Citizenship) and humanity without revealing their specific identity, preserving privacy in a transparent protocol.
*   **Meritocratic Stipends**: Citizens earn bonus voice credits each cycle based on their accumulated reputation, rewarding consistent expertise and participation.

### 6. Minimalist UX & Real-Time Engagement
*   **Consolidated UI**: Technical complexity (health logs, task monitoring) is nested within a single "System Governance" view to maintain a minimal, user-friendly dashboard for the average citizen.
*   **PWA & Push Notifications**: The frontend is a Progressive Web App (PWA) with hardened iconography and manifest support. It uses **Socket.io** and **Browser Notifications** to alert citizens to "Critical" governance events and emergency proposals in real-time.

### 7. Background Infrastructure
*   **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) integrated into the API server that triggers synchronization cycles and system health checks every 5 minutes, ensuring the repository operates on total autopilot.

***

The system has now stabilized at **v1.1.59**, featuring a complete end-to-end loop for voluntary funding, expert-driven verification, and privacy-preserving identity.

## LiquidGov: The Autonomous Voluntary State Architecture & Design Patterns

LiquidGov is a distributed governance protocol designed to replace coercive systems with a cognitive meritocracy. It is powered by voluntary participation, mathematical fairness, and verifiable accountability.

### 1. Core Infrastructure: The APEP Engine
The repository is managed by the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**, a modularized suite of engines located in `src/core/protocol/`. This system ensures the repository remains functional and synchronized without human intervention:
*   **NetworkSynchronizer**: Automates multi-remote git operations and recursive submodule tracking.
*   **MergeEngine (Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge)**: Implements a forward-merge (feature → main) and reverse-merge (main → feature) strategy to prevent architectural drift, especially in environments with multiple AI-generated branches.
*   **ValidationGatekeeper**: Enforces workspace integrity, ensuring mandatory documentation (VISION, MEMORY, ROADMAP) and script parity across Unix and Windows environments.
*   **AutonomousDeployer**: Stages verified artifacts into deployment directories.

### 2. State & Persistence Philosophy
*   **Persistent SQLite Store**: The system utilizes a relational SQLite schema (`src/models/Store.ts`) via `better-sqlite3`. This ensures that citizen identities, reputation histories, votes, contributions, and treasury ledgers persist across system restarts, moving away from volatile in-memory storage.
*   **Singleton Pattern**: Core logic engines (Identity, Governance, Triage, TaskManager) are exposed as global singletons, ensuring a "single source of truth" for the API server and the background watchdog services.

### 3. Identity: Privacy-Preserving Meritocracy
*   **Tiered Proof-of-Humanity (PoH)**: LiquidGov uses a hierarchical verification model (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock). Stronger verification methods programmatically overwrite weaker ones.
*   **ZKP Integration**: Integration with **Semaphore v4** allows citizens to prove humanity and group membership without revealing their specific identity, preserving privacy in a transparent governance model.
*   **Subject-Specific Reputation**: Reputation is not a single number but a record of expertise across subjects (e.g., "Infrastructure", "Privacy"). It is earned through successfully completing proposals or participating in juries.

### 4. Democratic & Fiscal Engines
*   **Quadratic Voting (QV)**: Used for decision-making (cost = votes²) to measure intensity of preference and protect minority interests.
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation**: A unique innovation where experts spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools. Power is not abstract; it is backed by the actual credits/capital of the citizens who trust the expert.
*   **Democratic Override**: A mathematical safeguard that retracts a delegate's proportional weight if the original delegator casts a personal vote on the same proposal, ensuring absolute citizen sovereignty.
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF)**: A voluntary taxation model where central matching pools (sourced from voluntary donations) amplify individual contributions using $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$ to maximize collective utility.

### 5. Accountability: The Milestone Escrow Loop
*   **Milestone-Based Release**: Funds are held in escrow and released incrementally.
*   **Proof of Work (PoW)**: Proposers must submit verifiable evidence (URLs/Hashes) for each milestone before funds are released.
*   **Weighted Juries**: Randomly assigned juries of verified humans verify PoW. **Subject-matter experts** (those with high Rep in the field) receive double voting weight, ensuring high-quality oversight.
*   **Dispute System**: Rejection by a jury triggers a dispute state, freezing capital and slashing the proposer's reputation.

### 6. UX & Accessibility
*   **Consolidated Governance**: The UI hides technical complexity (health logs, background tasks) within a single "System Governance" view for a minimal experience.
*   **PWA & Real-Time Notifications**: The system is a Progressive Web App (PWA) with hardened iconography. It uses **Socket.io** and **Browser Notifications** to alert citizens to "Critical" governance events and emergency proposals in real-time.

### 7. Background Infrastructure
*   **Autonomous Watchdog**: A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) integrated into the API server that triggers synchronization cycles and system health checks every 5 minutes.

***

The system is currently stable at **v1.1.59**, with a complete end-to-end loop for voluntary funding, expert-driven verification, and privacy-preserving identity.

---
**Summary of recent work:**
- Implemented **Milestone Proof of Work (PoW)** submission and jury verification UI.
- Integrated **Real-time Browser Notifications** for critical proposals.
- Hardened **PWA Infrastructure** with valid icon assets and manifest compliance.
- Resolved TypeScript CI failures in test suites.

The system is now fully functional as an **Autonomous Voluntary State**.

[PROJECT_MEMORY]

## LiquidGov: The Autonomous Voluntary State - Comprehensive Architectural Summary

LiquidGov is an autonomous governance protocol and "Voluntary State" infrastructure designed to facilitate expert-driven collective action through a cognitive meritocracy. It aims to replace coercive taxation and majoritarian tyranny with voluntary capital routing, mathematical fairness, and verifiable accountability.

### 1. Core Architectural Framework
*   **The APEP Engine:** The repository is managed by the **Autonomous Project Execution Protocol (APEP)**. This modular suite of engines (`src/core/protocol/`) automates synchronization, conflict-aware merging (Dual-Direction Intelligent Merge), and deployment, ensuring the repository remains functional without human intervention.
*   **Persistence Layer:** The system utilizes a **Persistent SQLite Store** (`src/models/Store.ts`) via `better-sqlite3`. This relational layer ensures that citizen identities, reputation histories, votes, contributions, and treasury ledgers persist across system restarts, moving away from early-stage volatile in-memory implementations.
*   **Monorepo Structure:** A unified TypeScript/Express backend and React/Vite frontend using Tailwind CSS v4.
*   **Singleton Pattern:** Core logic engines (Identity, Governance, Triage, TaskManager, Watchdog) are exposed as global singletons, ensuring state consistency across the API and background services.

### 2. The "Voluntary State" Fiscal Model
*   **Voluntary Tax Routing:** Citizens choose which "departments" to fund by depositing capital into **Subject-Specific Matching Pools** (e.g., "Infrastructure", "Privacy", "Roads").
*   **Quadratic Funding (QF):** Matching pools amplify individual contributions using the formula $(\sum \sqrt{c_i})^2 - \sum c_i$. This mathematically maximizes the utility of many small donors over a few large ones, ensuring fair and democratic capital allocation.
*   **Match Multipliers:** The system provides real-time "impact estimates" to citizens, showing how their voluntary contribution will be amplified by the matching pool.

### 3. Democratic Innovations: QV & Liquid Delegation
*   **Quadratic Voting (QV):** Used for all decision-making (cost = votes²) to measure intensity of preference while protecting minority interests.
*   **Credit-Backed Liquid Delegation:** A unique innovation where experts spend "voice credits" directly from their delegators' pools. Influence is not abstract; it is backed by the actual capital/credits of the trusting citizens, ensuring representatives have "skin in the game."
*   **Democratic Override:** A mathematical safeguard that detects when a delegator casts a personal vote on a proposal where their delegate has already acted. It retracts the delegate's proportional weight for that specific vote, ensuring absolute citizen sovereignty.

### 4. Accountability: Milestone-Based Governance
*   **Escrow Loop:** Funds for proposals are never released upfront. They are held in escrow and released incrementally upon milestone completion.
*   **Proof of Work (PoW):** Proposers must submit verifiable evidence (URLs, commits, or hashes) for each milestone via the Action Panel before funds are released.
*   **Expert-Weighted Juries:** Verification is performed by randomly assigned juries of verified humans. **Subject-matter experts** (those with Rep >= 10 in the field) receive double voting weight, ensuring high-quality oversight.
*   **Dispute & Slashing:** Rejection by a jury triggers a dispute state, freezing capital and slashing the proposer's reputation.

### 5. Identity & Sybil Resistance
*   **Tiered Proof-of-Humanity (PoH):** A hierarchical verification model (ZKP > Endorsement > External > Mock). Stronger verification methods programmatically overwrite weaker ones.
*   **ZKP Privacy:** Integration with **Semaphore v4** allows citizens to prove humanity and group membership without revealing their specific identity.
*   **Meritocratic Stipends:** Citizens earn bonus voice credits each cycle based on their accumulated subject-specific reputation, rewarding long-term constructive participation.
*   **Automated Decay:** Reputation slowly decays during periods of inactivity to ensure governance is led by current experts.

### 6. UX, PWA, and Real-Time Engagement
*   **Minimalist UI Philosophy:** Technical complexity (health logs, background tasks) is nested within a single "System Governance" view to maintain a clean dashboard.
*   **PWA Hardening:** The frontend is a Progressive Web App with hardened iconography and manifest support for mobile-first accessibility.
*   **Real-Time Notifications:** The system uses **Socket.io** and **Browser Notifications** to alert citizens to "Critical" governance events and emergency proposals in real-time.

### 7. Operational Autopilot
*   **Autonomous Watchdog:** A background service (`src/core/watchdog.ts`) integrated into the API server that triggers synchronization cycles and system health checks every 5 minutes, keeping the protocol on total autopilot.

***

The system is currently stable at **v1.1.59**, having successfully transitioned into a fully functional "Voluntary State" infrastructure with verified accountability and privacy-preserving identity.

---
**Recent Work Summary:**
- Implemented **Milestone PoW** submission/verification logic and UI.
- Integrated **Real-time Push Notifications** for critical proposals.
- Hardened **PWA Infrastructure** and assets.
- Resolved TypeScript CI strictness failures.

All core protocol tests and frontend builds are passing.
